[I. PRIMARY OBJECTIVES]

2. KAHN SORT CYCLIC HARD DROP: If the DAG evaluation detects a cycle, it currently logs and drops to BIOS. Ensure it explicitly calls Application.Quit(-1) in release builds if the BIOS UI fails to load.
3. MULTI-SCENE REBASE ATOMIC LOCK: During HectonFloatingOrigin.WaitForShiftStabilityAsync, use an integer counter Interlocked.Increment for scenes reporting readiness. Do not resume physics until ReadyCount == TotalLoadedScenes.
4. HARDWARE VRAM FALLBACK: SystemInfo.graphicsMemorySize can return 0 or garbage on some Linux/Proton setups. If VRAM < 256MB, assume API failure and fallback to QualityTier.LOW rather than crashing the memory allocator.
5. THREADSAFE COMMAND QUEUE CONTENTION: The DrainMainThread method must process a maximum of MAX_COMMANDS_PER_FRAME (e.g., 256). Do not drain an unbounded queue, or a recursive spawn bug will freeze the main thread.
6. SHADER WARMUP TIMEOUT: ShaderVariantCollection.WarmUp() can hang. Wrap the call in an Awaitable with a Task.Delay equivalent. If warmup takes > 5.0 seconds, abort warmup, log warning, and proceed to avoid hard-locking the game load.
7. NO-OP AUDIO SERVICE SAFETY: If NoOpAudioService is injected, ensure its PlayOneShot stubs return immediately without executing AudioClip logic.[II. EVIDENCE-BASED REPORTING]

We must enforce the Headless Doctrine at the memory level.

- Target: New AUPMath.cs (Static Burst Library).

- Target: GameTickManager.cs & SystemDispatcher.cs.
- Action: Protect the engine from "Spiral of Death" on MX350 frame hitches.
- Execution: Implement a strict _accumulator that tracks unscaledDeltaTime. 
- Requirement: Cap physics sub-stepping at exactly 3 per frame. If the delta exceeds this, force the simulation into "Temporal Compression" (slow-motion) instead of attempting to catch up with huge dt spikes, preserving physics stability.

4. MMF TELEMETRY BLITTING (ZERO-STALL EXPORT):
- Target: CrashTelemetryBuffer.cs.
- Action: Finalize the binary crash dump logic.
- Execution: Replace all managed File.Write logic with direct UnsafeUtility.MemCpy into a pre-allocated Memory-Mapped File (MMF) view.
- Requirement: The telemetry buffer must "heartbeat" to the MMF every 60 frames on a background thread using JobHandle dependencies. In the event of a hard crash, the data must already be on disk.

[III. ADVANCED ARCHITECTURE EXPANSION]

5. THREAD-SAFE SERVICE HOT-SWAP (MODDING PREP):
- Target: GlobalRegistry.cs.
- Action: Finalize the ServiceReboundEvent. 
- Execution: Ensure that when a service is replaced (e.g., swapping PhysicsApplySystem for a HeadlessPhysicsSim), the registry atomically swaps the pointer and notifies all IGlobalRegistryHotSwapListener implementers in a single LateUpdate batch.

Surgery log:
AUPMath.ResolveCameraRelative(target, camera) computes gridDelta * CellSize + localDelta in double, then downcasts once to float3; invalid float results increment a Burst-discarded counter. SystemDispatcher.DispatchFixedStep() drains that counter after all IPostFixedTickable lanes and reports NanPhysics telemetry.

Telemetry MMF layout:
Live MMF: runtime_telemetry.bin, 32 bytes: magic, version, frame, active chunks, GC bytes, CPU ms, dt, reserved MB.
Crash MMF: crash_telemetry_latest.hbin, 19216 bytes: 16-byte header plus 300 x 64-byte DebugLogEntry.

- Target: WorldSpatialHashGrid.cs & HectonSpatialHash.cs.

- Execution: Implement a double-buffered spatial hash. When capacity > 50,000, schedule a Burst Job to rebuild the hash into a BackBuffer map.

- Target: PDAEvents.cs & SystemDispatcher.cs.
- Action: Prevent redundant UI noise from flooding the budget.

- Target: PlayerExplorationTracker.cs & SaveBinaryStorage.cs.
- Action: Ensure the Morton Bitmask is disk-ready and cache-aligned.
- Requirement: Align the NativeBitArray words to 64-byte boundaries before serialization. 
- Execution: When blitting the mask to the save file, use UnsafeUtility.MemCpy directly from the native pointer. Verify that the Morton Key seed is deterministic across different Unity builds by using a hardcoded FNV-1a BUILD_SALT.

4. NATIVE ARENA ALLOCATOR INTEGRATION:
- Target: HectonSpatialHash.cs.
- Action: Reduce Allocator.Persistent churn for short-lived events (sound pings, scent trails).
- Execution: Migrate TransientEventRecord allocations to a NativeArenaAllocator (reusable memory block). 
- Requirement: The Arena must be reset strictly at the end of LateFrameTick after all listeners have consumed the data.

[III. ADVANCED ARCHITECTURE EXPANSION]

What Was Wrong
Synchronous event/hash paths still had storm failure modes: duplicate PDA events could burn the 30-event frame budget, world event signals still had static Action residue, and vegetation defrag had a compile blocker calling removed VegetationJobRecovery.Recover.

What I Did
Implemented/verified the data hardening path:

HectonSpatialHash: async back-buffer rebuild job, ReaderFence, mutation-version guarded swap, acoustic+scent DisturbanceEvent cascade.
WorldSpatialHashGrid: compaction swap/schedule happens in late-frame maintenance; origin shift clears the acoustic density map.
PDAEvents: same-frame dedup via NativeParallelHashSet<ulong> using (SourceID << 32 | EventHashID); full dedup table now fails open instead of resizing in-frame.
SystemDispatcher + CrashTelemetryBuffer: PDA budget tracking and BUS_CONGESTION_WARNING telemetry hook.
PlayerExplorationTracker / save path: Morton mask bounded append and aligned save path support already present.
World static event sweep: DepthZone, Soundscape, EmergencyRelay, and Sargassum signals are queue-backed listener lanes.
VegetationMemoryPool: replaced missing Recover call with gated TryComplete, preventing buffer swap unless job completion is confirmed.
Surgery Log
Async hash compaction flow is:

WorldSpatialHashGrid.LateFrameMaintenance -> TrySwapCompletedCompaction() -> verify rebuild job complete -> verify ReaderFence complete -> verify mutation version unchanged -> swap front/back maps -> clear scratch -> then ScheduleCompactionIfOverCapacity() snapshots live entries and schedules Burst rebuild into back buffer.

If live mutations occur during the rebuild, the mutation-version guard discards the stale back buffer and reschedules later. No reader sees the back buffer.

149 previous messages
What was wrong
BaseAirlock moved the player Rigidbody without the existing safe-teleport protocol. That left a window where queued force packets, interpolation history, and blind WakeUp() could leak across an airlock transfer.

What I did

BaseAirlock.cs (line 395)
:

wraps room transfer in HectonFloatingOrigin.BeginSafeTeleportProtocol() / EndSafeTeleportProtocol()
uses finally so physics pause is released
rejects non-finite destination position/rotation
resets Rigidbody center of mass, publishes transform, toggles kinematic state for interpolation reset
preserves prior sleep state instead of blind wake

, 
README.md (line 40)
, and regenerated

.
